# /work: measured component specifications

Source: https://www.sunnypatel.net/work

Measurements use CSS pixels, viewport height 1000px, DPR 1. Padding/margin order: top right bottom left. Values are literal Chromium computed values, including fractional pixels and OKLab colors. Hidden elements remain in the DOM: display and visibility are recorded; inherited styles do not prove visibility. A zero-size image may be lazy-loaded. Animation opacity/transform values are snapshots, not final-state specifications. Full per-element records, exact selectors, pseudo-elements and source classes are in the corresponding JSON files.
